    "body": "Release v0.11.0\n\n## Highlights\n\n*   **Flow control for P/D:** Saturation gating is stage-aware. Prefill and decode are measured separately and admission gates on the busier stage ([#2221](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2221)). The beta `max-saturation-detector` combines several detectors and gates on the highest one, and each child can be limited to prefill or decode ([#2827](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2827), [#2926](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2926)). Queue TTLs can be set per priority band, clients can ask for a shorter one with `x-llm-d-inference-ttl`, and `noEndpointRequestTTL` bounds the wait while the pool is empty ([#2649](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2649), [#2284](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2284)). New capacity utilization gauges and an `x-llm-d-flow-queue-duration-ms` response header ([#2184](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2184), [#2490](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2490)).\n*   **Scheduling and routing:** The new `latency-observation-scorer` ranks endpoints by TTFT measured from real responses ([#2371](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2371)). In P/D, load is split by role: input tokens count against prefill and estimated output tokens against decode ([#2396](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2396)). The `context-length-aware` scorer can route cached follow-ups by the prefill work that remains ([#2559](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2559)). Endpoints tied for the top score take turns instead of one pod getting the whole burst ([#2515](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2515), [#2709](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2709)). New `turn-priority` fairness strategy and endpoint attribute weight scorer ([#2116](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2116), [#2204](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2204)).\n*   **Tokenization and render:** `token-producer` renders Anthropic Messages natively through vLLM `/v1/messages/render`, and falls back to the old conversion when the endpoint is missing ([#2707](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2707)). It can balance render calls across discovered endpoints ([#2701](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2701)), and supports HTTPS, mTLS and render endpoints started with `--api-key` ([#2664](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2664), [#2678](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2678), [#2763](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2763)). Opt-in xxhash block hashing makes precise prefix hashing about 8x cheaper ([#2237](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2237)).\n*   **API coverage:** SGLang `/generate` ([#2391](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2391)), OpenAI text-to-speech ([#2484](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2484)), `/v1/images/edits` ([#2562](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2562)), and pre-tokenized batch prompts on `/v1/completions` ([#2523](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2523)). Paths no parser claims are forwarded to the model server by default through `passthrough-parser` ([#2507](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2507)). ATOM is a built-in engine type ([#2476](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2476)).\n*   **Coordinator and DisaggregatedSet:** The coordinator exposes Prometheus metrics, optionally over HTTPS, forwards unregistered paths such as `/v1/models` to a decode pod, and adds an optional async-broker step ([#2435](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2435), [#2752](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2752), [#2357](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2357), [#2325](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2325)). EPP replicas agree on DisaggregatedSet revision decisions, and the coordinator keeps the same decision across encode, prefill and decode ([#2570](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2570), [#2571](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2571), [#2572](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2572), [#2576](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2576)).\n*   **Operations:** A `--feature-gates` flag enables gates without restating the whole config ([#2388](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2388)). Helm adds Envoy priority routing for EPP failover in standalone mode, `router.epp.deploymentStrategy`, structured `router.epp.pluginsConfig` and `router.imagePullSecrets`, and restarts the EPP pod when its ConfigMaps change ([#2228](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2228), [#2603](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2603), [#2620](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2620), [#2766](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2766), [#2460](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2460)).\n*   **Observability:** Logs carry `trace_id` and `span_id`. New spans cover tokenization and the KV-event pipeline, and trace context is read from ext_proc gRPC metadata so traces connect end to end ([#2271](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2271), [#2272](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2272), [#2830](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2830)). Spans carry `service.name` and the pod resource attributes ([#2298](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2298)).\n*   **Fixes:** `golang.org/x/crypto` v0.55.0 for CVE-2026-56854 ([#2739](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2739)). Stale precise prefix-cache state after endpoints are removed ([#2824](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2824)). The PD Multi Tier decode leg starting before prefill stored its KV ([#2451](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2451)). `min_tokens` breaking the prefill leg ([#2593](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2593)). MoRI-IO concurrent dispatch ([#2816](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2816)). Published standalone charts pointing agentgateway at a missing image tag ([#2732](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2732)).\n*   **Precise prefix cache:** LoRA requests keep `BlockStored` extra keys aligned with request-side keys, restoring precise cache matches ([#2929](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2929)).\n\n## Upgrade Steps & Deprecations\n\nThis release removes many items deprecated in v0.7.0 and v0.9.0. Configurations that still use them fail at startup.\n\n*   **Removed plugins:** `pd-profile-handler`, use `disagg-profile-handler` with the nested `profiles` and `deciders` format ([#2509](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2509)). `precise-prefix-cache-scorer`, use `precise-prefix-cache-producer` plus `prefix-cache-scorer` with `prefixMatchInfoProducerName` ([#2844](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2844)). `by-label` and `by-label-selector`, use `label-selector-filter` or the role filters ([#2865](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2865)). `tokenizer`, use `token-producer` ([#2869](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2869)).\n*   **Removed config:** The top-level `saturationDetector` and `parser` fields, use `flowControl.saturationDetector` and `requestHandler.parsers` ([#2838](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2838)). The UDS tokenizer: `udsTokenizerConfig` and `indexerConfig.tokenizersPoolConfig` are rejected, set `vllm.url` on `token-producer` instead ([#2512](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2512)). `lasDecayFactor` on `program-aware-fairness`, decay uses `lasHalfLifeSeconds` only ([#2120](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2120)).\n*   **Pod label:** `llm-d.ai/role: both` no longer matches. Use `prefill-decode` ([#2509](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2509)).\n*   **Removed metrics:** The legacy `inference_extension_*`, `llm_d_inference_scheduler_*`, core request and inference-pool, and `inference_objective` predicted-latency families. Use the `llm_d_epp_*` series. Three `inference_extension_*` series stay for the workload-variant autoscaler ([#2826](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2826), [#2675](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2675), [#2821](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2821), [#2509](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2509)).\n*   **Changed metrics:** KV-event metrics use the `llm_d_epp` prefix instead of `llm_d_router_epp` ([#2385](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2385)). `kv_cache_index_lookup_hits_total` and `kv_cache_index_max_pod_hit_count_total` count only the longest contiguous prefix on one pod, so they read lower. Re-baseline dashboards and alerts ([#2400](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2400), [#2680](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2680)). An empty-pool expiry is labelled `EvictedNoEndpoints` instead of `EvictedTTL` ([#2284](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2284)).\n*   **Coordinator TLS on by default:** The inference listener serves HTTPS. Use https, or set `--secure-coordinator=false` to keep plaintext ([#2805](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2805)).\n*   **JSON logs:** Field names follow OpenTelemetry. Collectors that match on `msg` need to match `body` ([#2643](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2643)).\n*   **Tracing:** With tracing on and `OTEL_TRACES_EXPORTER` unset, spans go to OTLP instead of stdout ([#2566](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2566)).\n*   **Helm:** Charts default to Prometheus Operator monitoring for every gateway provider. GKE users who relied on implicit GMP monitoring must configure it explicitly ([#2374](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2374)). The Prometheus ClusterRole and binding are created only when metrics authentication is on ([#2798](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2798)).\n*   **Endpoint attribute plugins:** `endpoint-attribute-filter` and `endpoint-attribute-scorer` take a separate `producer` parameter, and the combined `Name/Producer` form is rejected. Out-of-tree plugins that use `AttributeMap` need updating ([#2190](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2190)).\n*   **Prefix scoring:** Device tiers without a configured weight score 0 instead of 1.0 ([#2809](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2809)).\n*   **Stricter startup checks:** Optional dependency cycles and invalid plugin ordering fail startup ([#2721](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2721)). Non-positive `--metrics-staleness-threshold` and `--refresh-prometheus-metrics-interval` are rejected ([#2914](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2914)).\n*   **Conditional decode:** The `Prefer: if-available` 412 gate moved into `prefix-based-pd-decider`. Without that plugin, such requests get 412. Set `nonCachedTokens: 1` to keep the old behavior ([#2335](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2335)).\n*   **P/D failures:** When a required prefill stage finds no endpoint, the request fails instead of running prefill on the decode pod ([#2521](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2521)). No eligible decode endpoint returns 503 `rejected-no-endpoints` ([#2631](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2631)).\n*   **Deprecated:** `dataLayer.discovery.pluginRef`, use `dataLayer.discovery.endpoints.pluginRef`; old configs are migrated automatically ([#2434](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2434)). `kvcache.KVBlockScorer` and `kvcache.NewTracedScorer` ([#2669](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2669)).\n*   **Latency predictor:** Charts from this release use the `v0.8.0` latency predictor images, the latest published predictor release.\n\n## Known Issues\n\n*   Endpoint score emission (`--emit-endpoint-scores`) emits nothing under the deprecated `data-parallel-profile-handler`.\n*   With KV-event replay disabled, an inference engine that restarts at the same endpoint can retain stale precise prefix-cache state until eviction. Follow [#2853](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/issues/2853) and [#2854](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2854).\n\n## What's Changed\n\n<details>\n<summary><b>Click to expand</b></summary>\n\n* fix(kvevents): drop the LoRA name from BlockStored extra keys by @wseaton in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2929\n* test(flowcontrol): eviction dynamics benchmark by @LukeAVanDrie in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2107\n* refactor(datalayer): key AttributeMap by DataKey instead of string by @satyamg1620 in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2190\n* Filter prefix cache events by cache group kind by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2363\n* docs: add release-note fragment for PR #2190 by @llm-d-router-release-notes[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2367\n* fix(charts): emit leading document separators in monitoring partials by @LukeAVanDrie in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2368\n* Bumped Go version directive from 1.26.5 to 1.26.6. by @ahg-g in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2386\n* Unify the metrics subsystem on llm_d_epp by @ahg-g in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2385\n* fix(flowcontrol): prevent aggregate stats underflow on flow GC by @LukeAVanDrie in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2382\n* docs: add release-note fragment for PR #2385 by @llm-d-router-release-notes[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2387\n* feat: EPP peer discovery infrastructure by @evacchi in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2341\n* make metricsLoggingInterval values match the comment by @yamt in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2405\n* fix(tokenizer): translate Anthropic tool calls and thinking for render by @mschulist in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2389\n* Bound and resume KV-event replay by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2410\n* fix(options): validate refresh-metrics-interval 50ms floor at startup by @noalimoy in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2394\n* deps(actions): bump github/codeql-action/upload-sarif from 4.37.6 to 4.37.7 in the github-actions group by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2418\n* deps(go): bump google.golang.org/protobuf from 1.36.12-0.20260120151049-f2248ac996af to 1.36.12 in the go-dependencies group by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2417\n* docs: add release-note fragment for PR #2389 by @llm-d-router-release-notes[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2413\n* test(tokenizer): cover Anthropic tool render request by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2412\n* perf(flowcontrol): index active queues so dispatch scans O(active) flows by @LukeAVanDrie in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2120\n* feat(flowcontrol): split queue-wait budget by unavailability regime by @satyamg1620 in     "body": "Release v0.11.0-rc.2\n\n## Highlights\n\n* Fix precise prefix-cache matching for LoRA requests by keeping `BlockStored` extra keys aligned with request-side keys ([#2929](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2929)).\n\n## Known Issues\n\n* With KV-event replay disabled, an inference engine that restarts at the same endpoint can retain stale precise prefix-cache state until eviction. Follow [#2853](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/issues/2853) and [#2854](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2854).\n\n## What's Changed\n\n* fix(kvevents): drop the LoRA name from BlockStored extra keys by @wseaton in [#2929](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2929)\n\n**Full Changelog**: https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/compare/v0.11.0-rc.1...v0.11.0-rc.2\n",
    "mentions_count": 1
  },

        "digest": "sha256:aa85ed4b39210b43e7366472624761b626332633572adbea06dc324b2d6762f2",
        "download_count": 3,
        "created_at": "2026-09-18T20:41:18Z",
        "updated_at": "2026-09-18T20:41:18Z",

    "tarball_url": "https://api.github.com/repos/llm-d/llm-d-router/tarball/v0.11.0-rc.1",
    "zipball_url": "https://api.github.com/repos/llm-d/llm-d-router/zipball/v0.11.0-rc.1",
    "body": "Release v0.11.0-rc.1\n\n## Highlights\n\n*   **Flow control for P/D:** Saturation gating is stage-aware. Prefill and decode are measured separately and admission gates on the busier stage ([#2221](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2221)). The beta `max-saturation-detector` combines several detectors and gates on the highest one, and each child can be limited to prefill or decode ([#2827](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2827), [#2926](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2926)). Queue TTLs can be set per priority band, clients can ask for a shorter one with `x-llm-d-inference-ttl`, and `noEndpointRequestTTL` bounds the wait while the pool is empty ([#2649](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2649), [#2284](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2284)). New capacity utilization gauges and an `x-llm-d-flow-queue-duration-ms` response header ([#2184](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2184), [#2490](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2490)).\n*   **Scheduling and routing:** The new `latency-observation-scorer` ranks endpoints by TTFT measured from real responses ([#2371](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2371)). In P/D, load is split by role: input tokens count against prefill and estimated output tokens against decode ([#2396](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2396)). The `context-length-aware` scorer can route cached follow-ups by the prefill work that remains ([#2559](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2559)). Endpoints tied for the top score take turns instead of one pod getting the whole burst ([#2515](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2515), [#2709](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2709)). New `turn-priority` fairness strategy and endpoint attribute weight scorer ([#2116](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2116), [#2204](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2204)).\n*   **Tokenization and render:** `token-producer` renders Anthropic Messages natively through vLLM `/v1/messages/render`, and falls back to the old conversion when the endpoint is missing ([#2707](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2707)). It can balance render calls across discovered endpoints ([#2701](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2701)), and supports HTTPS, mTLS and render endpoints started with `--api-key` ([#2664](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2664), [#2678](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2678), [#2763](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2763)). Opt-in xxhash block hashing makes precise prefix hashing about 8x cheaper ([#2237](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2237)).\n*   **API coverage:** SGLang `/generate` ([#2391](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2391)), OpenAI text-to-speech ([#2484](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2484)), `/v1/images/edits` ([#2562](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2562)), and pre-tokenized batch prompts on `/v1/completions` ([#2523](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2523)). Paths no parser claims are forwarded to the model server by default through `passthrough-parser` ([#2507](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2507)). ATOM is a built-in engine type ([#2476](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2476)).\n*   **Coordinator and DisaggregatedSet:** The coordinator exposes Prometheus metrics, optionally over HTTPS, forwards unregistered paths such as `/v1/models` to a decode pod, and adds an optional async-broker step ([#2435](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2435), [#2752](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2752), [#2357](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2357), [#2325](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2325)). EPP replicas agree on DisaggregatedSet revision decisions, and the coordinator keeps the same decision across encode, prefill and decode ([#2570](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2570), [#2571](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2571), [#2572](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2572), [#2576](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2576)).\n*   **Operations:** A `--feature-gates` flag enables gates without restating the whole config ([#2388](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2388)). Helm adds Envoy priority routing for EPP failover in standalone mode, `router.epp.deploymentStrategy`, structured `router.epp.pluginsConfig` and `router.imagePullSecrets`, and restarts the EPP pod when its ConfigMaps change ([#2228](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2228), [#2603](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2603), [#2620](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2620), [#2766](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2766), [#2460](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2460)).\n*   **Observability:** Logs carry `trace_id` and `span_id`. New spans cover tokenization and the KV-event pipeline, and trace context is read from ext_proc gRPC metadata so traces connect end to end ([#2271](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2271), [#2272](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2272), [#2830](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2830)). Spans carry `service.name` and the pod resource attributes ([#2298](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2298)).\n*   **Fixes:** `golang.org/x/crypto` v0.55.0 for CVE-2026-56854 ([#2739](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2739)). Stale precise prefix-cache state after endpoints are removed ([#2824](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2824)). The PD Multi Tier decode leg starting before prefill stored its KV ([#2451](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2451)). `min_tokens` breaking the prefill leg ([#2593](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2593)). MoRI-IO concurrent dispatch ([#2816](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2816)). Published standalone charts pointing agentgateway at a missing image tag ([#2732](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2732)).\n\n## Upgrade Steps & Deprecations\n\nThis release removes many items deprecated in v0.7.0 and v0.9.0. Configurations that still use them fail at startup.\n\n*   **Removed plugins:** `pd-profile-handler`, use `disagg-profile-handler` with the nested `profiles` and `deciders` format ([#2509](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2509)). `precise-prefix-cache-scorer`, use `precise-prefix-cache-producer` plus `prefix-cache-scorer` with `prefixMatchInfoProducerName` ([#2844](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2844)). `by-label` and `by-label-selector`, use `label-selector-filter` or the role filters ([#2865](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2865)). `tokenizer`, use `token-producer` ([#2869](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2869)).\n*   **Removed config:** The top-level `saturationDetector` and `parser` fields, use `flowControl.saturationDetector` and `requestHandler.parsers` ([#2838](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2838)). The UDS tokenizer: `udsTokenizerConfig` and `indexerConfig.tokenizersPoolConfig` are rejected, set `vllm.url` on `token-producer` instead ([#2512](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2512)). `lasDecayFactor` on `program-aware-fairness`, decay uses `lasHalfLifeSeconds` only ([#2120](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2120)).\n*   **Pod label:** `llm-d.ai/role: both` no longer matches. Use `prefill-decode` ([#2509](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2509)).\n*   **Removed metrics:** The legacy `inference_extension_*`, `llm_d_inference_scheduler_*`, core request and inference-pool, and `inference_objective` predicted-latency families. Use the `llm_d_epp_*` series. Three `inference_extension_*` series stay for the workload-variant autoscaler ([#2826](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2826), [#2675](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2675), [#2821](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2821), [#2509](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2509)).\n*   **Changed metrics:** KV-event metrics use the `llm_d_epp` prefix instead of `llm_d_router_epp` ([#2385](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2385)). `kv_cache_index_lookup_hits_total` and `kv_cache_index_max_pod_hit_count_total` count only the longest contiguous prefix on one pod, so they read lower. Re-baseline dashboards and alerts ([#2400](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2400), [#2680](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2680)). An empty-pool expiry is labelled `EvictedNoEndpoints` instead of `EvictedTTL` ([#2284](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2284)).\n*   **Coordinator TLS on by default:** The inference listener serves HTTPS. Use https, or set `--secure-coordinator=false` to keep plaintext ([#2805](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2805)).\n*   **JSON logs:** Field names follow OpenTelemetry. Collectors that match on `msg` need to match `body` ([#2643](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2643)).\n*   **Tracing:** With tracing on and `OTEL_TRACES_EXPORTER` unset, spans go to OTLP instead of stdout ([#2566](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2566)).\n*   **Helm:** Charts default to Prometheus Operator monitoring for every gateway provider. GKE users who relied on implicit GMP monitoring must configure it explicitly ([#2374](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2374)). The Prometheus ClusterRole and binding are created only when metrics authentication is on ([#2798](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2798)).\n*   **Endpoint attribute plugins:** `endpoint-attribute-filter` and `endpoint-attribute-scorer` take a separate `producer` parameter, and the combined `Name/Producer` form is rejected. Out-of-tree plugins that use `AttributeMap` need updating ([#2190](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2190)).\n*   **Prefix scoring:** Device tiers without a configured weight score 0 instead of 1.0 ([#2809](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2809)).\n*   **Stricter startup checks:** Optional dependency cycles and invalid plugin ordering fail startup ([#2721](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2721)). Non-positive `--metrics-staleness-threshold` and `--refresh-prometheus-metrics-interval` are rejected ([#2914](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2914)).\n*   **Conditional decode:** The `Prefer: if-available` 412 gate moved into `prefix-based-pd-decider`. Without that plugin, such requests get 412. Set `nonCachedTokens: 1` to keep the old behavior ([#2335](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2335)).\n*   **P/D failures:** When a required prefill stage finds no endpoint, the request fails instead of running prefill on the decode pod ([#2521](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2521)). No eligible decode endpoint returns 503 `rejected-no-endpoints` ([#2631](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2631)).\n*   **Deprecated:** `dataLayer.discovery.pluginRef`, use `dataLayer.discovery.endpoints.pluginRef`; old configs are migrated automatically ([#2434](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2434)). `kvcache.KVBlockScorer` and `kvcache.NewTracedScorer` ([#2669](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2669)).\n*   **Latency predictor:** Charts from this release use the `v0.8.0` latency predictor images, the latest published predictor release.\n\n## Known Issues\n\n*   Endpoint score emission (`--emit-endpoint-scores`) emits nothing under the deprecated `data-parallel-profile-handler`.\n\n## What's     "body": "Release v0.10.0\r\n\r\n## Highlights\r\n\r\n*   **Disaggregated prefill and decode:** RDMA-free KV transfer through the new `offloading` KV connector on the sidecar, now with data-parallel support and a configurable P2P tier port ([#1888](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1888), [#2075](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2075), [#1937](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1937)). Native DisaggregatedSet revision routing for consistent rolling P/D updates ([#2141](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2141)). Load-proportional and CPU-tier-aware pull-source selection to avoid transfer hot spots ([#2032](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2032), [#2321](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2321)).\r\n*   **Scheduling and routing:** GPU utilization-aware routing via the DCGM extractor and the generic endpoint-attribute filter and scorer ([#1904](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1904)). The `header-profile-handler` selects one scheduling profile per request from a header ([#2176](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2176)). The `utilization-filter` (formerly active-request-filter) caps endpoints on any combination of active, running, waiting, and KV-cache utilization ([#2218](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2218)). New priority-aware stepped gating and soft-reflective-ceiling policies ([#1592](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1592), [#1956](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1956)), and a new `session_id` session-affinity strategy ([#2238](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2238)).\r\n*   **Multimodal:** OpenAI `/v1/images/generations` routing for diffusion backends such as vLLM-Omni ([#1936](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1936)), and video input in approximate prefix match ([#1911](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1911)).\r\n*   **Observability and security:** OpenTelemetry spans over the scoring path and end-to-end P/D trace propagation ([#1834](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1834), [#1667](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1667)). mTLS for the EPP metrics endpoint and scrape sources, plus configurable ext_proc TLS minimum version and cipher suites ([#1919](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1919), [#1858](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1858), [#2230](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2230)). Bounded metric cardinality for model-name and fairness-id labels ([#1909](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1909), [#2119](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2119)).\r\n*   **Coordinator and availability:** the Coordinator serves `/inference/v1/generate` ([#2077](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2077)). Non-leader EPP replicas keep their datastore populated so a request reaching a standby routes instead of returning 503, and `--drain-timeout` adds graceful shutdown ([#1884](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1884), [#1885](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1885)). Plugin stability levels gate alpha plugins behind `--allow-experimental-plugins` ([#2073](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2073)). KV-event replay is bounded and resumable across a dropped response ([#2410](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2410)).\r\n\r\n## Upgrade Steps & Deprecations\r\n\r\n*   **Action required, out-of-tree UsageLimitPolicy plugins:** `ComputeLimit` now fills a framework-provided ceilings buffer instead of returning a slice ([#2171](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2171)).\r\n*   **Action required, P2P key rename:** the sidecar `kv_transfer_params` keys are renamed to match the vLLM OffloadingConnector branch. Upgrade the sidecar and the engine together, since a mixed pair silently disables cached-prefix pulls ([#2140](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2140)).\r\n*   **Flow control bounded by default:** `defaultRequestTTL` defaults to 60s and per-band `maxRequests` to 5000. Scale-to-zero deployments should raise `defaultRequestTTL` above their cold-start time ([#2123](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2123)). Rejection codes now distinguish an empty pool (503) from retryable backpressure (429) ([#1751](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1751), [#2047](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2047)).\r\n*   **Config renames:** the flow-control queue `MaxMinHeap` is now `PriorityQueue`, and the QueueCapability system and FCFS ListQueue are removed ([#1690](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1690), [#1831](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1831)). The active-request-filter plugin is now `utilization-filter` ([#2218](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2218)).\r\n*   **Removed flags:** pd-sidecar flags deprecated since v0.7.0 are removed; use `--kv-connector`, `--enable-tls`, and `--tls-insecure-skip-verify` ([#2054](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2054)). `--vllm-port` is deprecated in favor of `--model-server-port` ([#2142](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2142)). The deprecated data-layer feature gate is removed ([#1728](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1728)).\r\n*   **Metric renames:** EPP KV metrics move to the `llm_d_router_epp` subsystem, with the former `kvcache_*` names kept as deprecated aliases. Update dashboards and alerts ([#1886](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1886)).\r\n*   **Publishing change:** the `-dev` charts and images are no longer published. Use the base charts with `--version v0` and the `:main` image tags ([#2048](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2048)).\r\n*   `samplingMean` and `maxDecodeTokenSamplesForPrediction` are deprecated and ignored ([#2037](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2037)). On prefill endpoints, `InFlightLoad.Requests` now drops at first token, which changes any custom config that scores or filters on it ([#2243](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2243)).\r\n\r\n## Known Issues\r\n\r\n*   Endpoint score emission (`--emit-endpoint-scores`) emits nothing under the deprecated data-parallel-profile-handler, or under pd-profile-handler when `primaryPort` is set ([#2004](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2004)).\r\n\r\n## What's Changed\r\n\r\n<details>\r\n<summary><b>Click to expand</b></summary>\r\n\r\n* docs: add release-note fragment for PR #1657 by @llm-d-router-release-notes[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1660\r\n* release: configure and pass LATENCY_PREDICTOR_TAG via Makefile by @liu-cong in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1675\r\n* fix(sidecar): inject W3C trace context on outbound proxy requests by @gyliu513 in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1667\r\n* Add operations.md guide for right sizing EPP container resources by @jtechapps in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1616\r\n* deps(go): bump llm-d-kv-cache to v0.9.0 by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1691\r\n* fix(sidecar): allowlist selector parsing for GA InferencePool API by @pierDipi in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1696\r\n* fix(standalone): require modelServers.matchLabels when InferencePool is disabled by @Jwrede in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1695\r\n* test: consolidate and rename e2e runner scripts by @roytman in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1698\r\n* feat(standalone): align agentgateway configuration with upstream GAIE PR 2905 by @abhay1999 in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1600\r\n* fix(scripts): support single quotes in image check by @weizhoublue in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1676\r\n* fix: resolve mismatched yq path for nested router subchart values by @weizhoublue in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1683\r\n* docs: add release-note fragment for PR #1616 by @llm-d-router-release-notes[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1687\r\n* docs: add release-note fragment for PR #1667 by @llm-d-router-release-notes[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1685\r\n* ci: propagate HF_TOKEN to e2e tests to avoid HF Hub rate limits by @Jwrede in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1572\r\n* ci: publish CRD manifests to release assets by @liu-cong in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1713\r\n* deps(actions): bump the github-actions group across 1 directory with 3 updates by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1715\r\n* deps(go): bump github.com/jellydator/ttlcache/v3 from 3.4.0 to 3.4.1     "tarball_url": "https://api.github.com/repos/llm-d/llm-d-router/tarball/v0.10.0-rc.1",

    "body": "Release v0.10.0-rc.1\r\n\r\n## Highlights\r\n\r\n*   **Disaggregated prefill and decode:** RDMA-free KV transfer through the new `offloading` KV connector on the sidecar, now with data-parallel support and a configurable P2P tier port ([#1888](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1888), [#2075](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2075), [#1937](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1937)). Native DisaggregatedSet revision routing for consistent rolling P/D updates ([#2141](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2141)). Load-proportional and CPU-tier-aware pull-source selection to avoid transfer hot spots ([#2032](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2032), [#2321](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2321)).\r\n*   **Scheduling and routing:** GPU utilization-aware routing via the DCGM extractor and the generic endpoint-attribute filter and scorer ([#1904](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1904)). The `header-profile-handler` selects one scheduling profile per request from a header ([#2176](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2176)). The `utilization-filter` (formerly active-request-filter) caps endpoints on any combination of active, running, waiting, and KV-cache utilization ([#2218](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2218)). New priority-aware stepped gating and soft-reflective-ceiling policies ([#1592](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1592), [#1956](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1956)), and a new `session_id` session-affinity strategy ([#2238](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2238)).\r\n*   **Multimodal:** OpenAI `/v1/images/generations` routing for diffusion backends such as vLLM-Omni ([#1936](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1936)), and video input in approximate prefix match ([#1911](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1911)).\r\n*   **Observability and security:** OpenTelemetry spans over the scoring path and end-to-end P/D trace propagation ([#1834](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1834), [#1667](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1667)). mTLS for the EPP metrics endpoint and scrape sources, plus configurable ext_proc TLS minimum version and cipher suites ([#1919](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1919), [#1858](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1858), [#2230](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2230)). Bounded metric cardinality for model-name and fairness-id labels ([#1909](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1909), [#2119](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2119)).\r\n*   **Coordinator and availability:** the Coordinator serves `/inference/v1/generate` ([#2077](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2077)). Non-leader EPP replicas keep their datastore populated so a request reaching a standby routes instead of returning 503, and `--drain-timeout` adds graceful shutdown ([#1884](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1884), [#1885](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1885)). Plugin stability levels gate alpha plugins behind `--allow-experimental-plugins` ([#2073](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2073)).\r\n\r\n## Upgrade Steps & Deprecations\r\n\r\n*   **Action required, out-of-tree UsageLimitPolicy plugins:** `ComputeLimit` now fills a framework-provided ceilings buffer instead of returning a slice ([#2171](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2171)).\r\n*   **Action required, P2P key rename:** the sidecar `kv_transfer_params` keys are renamed to match the vLLM OffloadingConnector branch. Upgrade the sidecar and the engine together, since a mixed pair silently disables cached-prefix pulls ([#2140](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2140)).\r\n*   **Flow control bounded by default:** `defaultRequestTTL` defaults to 60s and per-band `maxRequests` to 5000. Scale-to-zero deployments should raise `defaultRequestTTL` above their cold-start time ([#2123](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2123)). Rejection codes now distinguish an empty pool (503) from retryable backpressure (429) ([#1751](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1751), [#2047](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2047)).\r\n*   **Config renames:** the flow-control queue `MaxMinHeap` is now `PriorityQueue`, and the QueueCapability system and FCFS ListQueue are removed ([#1690](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1690), [#1831](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1831)). The active-request-filter plugin is now `utilization-filter` ([#2218](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2218)).\r\n*   **Removed flags:** pd-sidecar flags deprecated since v0.7.0 are removed; use `--kv-connector`, `--enable-tls`, and `--tls-insecure-skip-verify` ([#2054](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2054)). `--vllm-port` is deprecated in favor of `--model-server-port` ([#2142](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2142)). The deprecated data-layer feature gate is removed ([#1728](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1728)).\r\n*   **Metric renames:** EPP KV metrics move to the `llm_d_router_epp` subsystem, with the former `kvcache_*` names kept as deprecated aliases. Update dashboards and alerts ([#1886](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1886)).\r\n*   **Publishing change:** the `-dev` charts and images are no longer published. Use the base charts with `--version v0` and the `:main` image tags ([#2048](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2048)).\r\n*   `samplingMean` and `maxDecodeTokenSamplesForPrediction` are deprecated and ignored ([#2037](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2037)). On prefill endpoints, `InFlightLoad.Requests` now drops at first token, which changes any custom config that scores or filters on it ([#2243](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2243)).\r\n\r\n## Known Issues\r\n\r\n*   Endpoint score emission (`--emit-endpoint-scores`) emits nothing under the deprecated data-parallel-profile-handler, or under pd-profile-handler when `primaryPort` is set ([#2004](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/2004)).\r\n\r\n## What's Changed\r\n\r\n<details>\r\n<summary><b>Click to expand</b></summary>\r\n\r\n* docs: add release-note fragment for PR #1657 by @llm-d-router-release-notes[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1660\r\n* release: configure and pass LATENCY_PREDICTOR_TAG via Makefile by @liu-cong in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1675\r\n* fix(sidecar): inject W3C trace context on outbound proxy requests by @gyliu513 in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1667\r\n* Add operations.md guide for right sizing EPP container resources by @jtechapps in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1616\r\n* deps(go): bump llm-d-kv-cache to v0.9.0 by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1691\r\n* fix(sidecar): allowlist selector parsing for GA InferencePool API by @pierDipi in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1696\r\n* fix(standalone): require modelServers.matchLabels when InferencePool is disabled by @Jwrede in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1695\r\n* test: consolidate and rename e2e runner scripts by @roytman in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1698\r\n* feat(standalone): align agentgateway configuration with upstream GAIE PR 2905 by @abhay1999 in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1600\r\n* fix(scripts): support single quotes in image check by @weizhoublue in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1676\r\n* fix: resolve mismatched yq path for nested router subchart values by @weizhoublue in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1683\r\n* docs: add release-note fragment for PR #1616 by @llm-d-router-release-notes[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1687\r\n* docs: add release-note fragment for PR #1667 by @llm-d-router-release-notes[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1685\r\n* ci: propagate HF_TOKEN to e2e tests to avoid HF Hub rate limits by @Jwrede in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1572\r\n* ci: publish CRD manifests to release assets by @liu-cong in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1713\r\n* deps(actions): bump the github-actions group across 1 directory with 3 updates by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1715\r\n* deps(go): bump github.com/jellydator/ttlcache/v3 from 3.4.0 to 3.4.1 in the go-dependencies group by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1709\r\n* fix: bump golang.org/x/crypto to v0.52.0 to resolve Trivy CVEs by @liu-cong in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1720\r\n* refactor(flowcontrol): single-ended SafeQueue backed by container/heap by @LukeAVanDrie in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1690\r\n* docs: add release-note fragment for PR #1690 by @llm-d-router-release-notes[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1725\r\n* chore: switch default vllm port from 8001 to 8200 for sidecar by @zdtsw in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1723\r\n* Fix inotify pre-flight check for PROM_ENABLED on macOS by @roytman in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1654\r\n* feat(inflightloadproducer): allow configuring output ratio by @asharkhan3101 in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1731\r\n* docs: add release-note fragment for PR #1731 by @llm-d-router-release-notes[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1743\r\n* feat: Enable the disabling of feature gates that are by default on/active by @shmuelk in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1728\r\n* fix(metrics): reset flow control metrics and correct deprecated references by @ishwar170695 in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1733\r\n* fix(flowcontrol): resolve ManagedQueue eagerly and fix FlowKey.String by @RishabhSaini in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1747\r\n* Update release instruction for fetching the existing release branch by @liu-cong in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1716\r\n* docs: assemble release notes for v0.9.0 by @llm-d-router-release-notes[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1688\r\n* docs: add release-note fragment for PR #1728 by @llm-d-router-release-notes[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1744\r\n* fix(inflightload): prevent in-flight counter underflow on endpoint flap by @LukeAVanDrie in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1739\r\n* fix(flowcontrol): return 503 (not 429) when no endpoints exist by @LukeAVanDrie in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1751\r\n* feat(inflightload): bound output-token estimate by client and operator caps by @LukeAVanDrie in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1750\r\n* docs: add release-note fragment for PR #1739 by @llm-d-router-release-notes[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1829\r\n* docs: add     "body": "Release v0.9.0\r\n\r\n## 1. Highlights\r\n*   **Performance Wins:** Drastically reduced memory allocations in `Scheduler.Schedule` by ~90% on large fleets ([PR #1171](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1171)) and optimized response usage parsing in the OpenAI parser ([PR #1583](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1583)).\r\n*   **Key Features & Additions:** \r\n    *   Added an approximate prefix-cache estimation feature for the Anthropic `v1/messages` API, including tools support ([PR #1508](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1508), [PR #1554](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1554)).\r\n    *   Introduced a new `FileDiscovery` plugin to handle YAML/JSON file loading and `fsnotify` file watching ([PR #1135](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1135)).\r\n    *   Enabled all HTTP parsers by default in the EPP ([PR #1488](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1488)) and added native metrics mapping for standard NVIDIA Triton Inference Server workloads ([PR #1207](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1207)).\r\n    *   Added stream-aware Time-to-First-Token (TTFT) and Inter-Token Latency (ITL) metrics ([PR #1499](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1499), [PR #1591](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1591)).\r\n*   **Key Bug Fixes:** Resolved a critical memory bound issue by clamping autotuned `blockSizeTokens` in the EPP indexer ([PR #1160](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1160)), fixed a ZMQ port range validation issue to prevent subscriber startup failures on multi-rank pods ([PR #1242](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1242)), and secured TLS defaults by isolating HTTP clients ([PR #1395](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1395)).\r\n\r\n## 2. Upgrade Steps & Deprecations\r\n*   **Breaking Flag & CRD Deletions:** \r\n    *   Removed the deprecated `--inference-pool-name` and `--inference-pool-namespace` flags from the sidecar ([PR #1416](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1416)).\r\n*   **Configuration Changes:** \r\n    *   EPP-managed headers have been renamed to follow the `x-llm-d` convention ([PR #1161](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1161)).\r\n    *   Flow control and request handler configuration parameters have been consolidated inside the config API ([PR #1247](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1247)).\r\n*   **Metric System Changes:** The legacy metrics feature gate and `pkg/epp/backend/metrics` have been entirely removed ([PR #1418](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1418)). Subsystem metrics names have been unified and renamed to `llm_d_epp` ([PR #1661](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1661)), and all EPP span attributes are now normalized under the `llm_d.epp.*` namespace ([PR #1670](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1670)).\r\n*   **Token Producer Backend Deprecation:** Deprecated the UDS-backend in `token-producer` in favor of the newer vLLM render endpoint ([PR #1079](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1079)).\r\n\r\n\r\n## What's Changed\r\n\r\n<details>\r\n<summary><b>Click to expand</b></summary>\r\n\r\n* deps(go): bump the kubernetes group with 5 updates by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/853\r\n* [migration] Clear GAIE destination paths of conflicting Go files by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/855\r\n* lint: simplify golangci config for GAIE migration by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/859\r\n* move scheduler pd unittests to the new locations by @roytman in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/849\r\n* migrate: sigs.k8s.io/gateway-api-inference-extension @ a048a796 by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/870\r\n* Added datalayer plugins docs by @ahg-g in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/875\r\n* fix image build with Podman by @roytman in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/869\r\n* use endpoints extractor for kvevents subscription mgmt in `precise-prefix-cache-scorer` by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/862\r\n* fix: CVE-2025-61729 - pin toolchain to go1.25.8 by @andresllh in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/861\r\n* fix: align pod label selector to kebab-case inference-serving by @yankay in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/854\r\n* move sidecar/latencypredictorasync into predictedlatency by @kaushikmitr in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/881\r\n* feat(epp): allow Parser.ParseRequest to signal skip and fallback to random endpoint by @zetxqx in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/882\r\n* fix(test): eliminate data race in SGLang connector test by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/886\r\n* Fix: dispatch endpoint events when no PollingDataSource is configured by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/879\r\n* Exclude envoyproxy.io from markdown link checker by @roytman in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/865\r\n* Add FULL_DUPLEX_STREAMED requirement by @roytman in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/892\r\n* updates to go.mod changes not included by latencypredictor client by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/897\r\n* deps(actions): bump the github-actions group with 2 updates by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/903\r\n* migrate: Correct some of the lint issues with the migrated IGW code by @shmuelk in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/909\r\n* add disk cleanup before e2e tests by @roytman in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/915\r\n* Refactoring and unification of e2e tests and deployment yaml files by @roytman in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/811\r\n* refactor: introduce ExtractorBase to remove forced no-op Extract from event-driven extractors by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/880\r\n* refactor: migrate GAIE runtime strings to llm-d.ai by @evacchi in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/922\r\n* refactor: align eviction plugin Type names with naming conventions by @jia-gao in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/894\r\n* fix make test-unit on arm64 by @roytman in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/940\r\n* Enabled more lint rules and corrected related code issues by @shmuelk in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/938\r\n* feat: Add Vertex AI parser support by @zetxqx in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/884\r\n* Add documentation to the plugins. by @revit13 in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/888\r\n* feat(datalayer): additive ensureDataLayer with InjectDefaults opt-out by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/941\r\n* migration: Migrate portions of the IGW Makefile by @shmuelk in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/887\r\n* [Migration]: Move GAIE deployment assets by @danehans in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/950\r\n* [Migration]: sigs.k8s.io/gateway-api-inference-extension @ 8ed5a0cd by @hexfusion in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/927\r\n* improve metrics logging by @andreyod in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/966\r\n* fix: make eviction runtime a builtin, not a plugin by @abdallahsamabd in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/898\r\n* feat(datalayer): plugin-driven source/extractor registration via Registrant interface by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/942\r\n* feat: Add pluggable DiscoveryPlugin interface by @ezrasilvera in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/908\r\n* refactor(disagg): add PreRequest to disagg-profile-handler, deprecate disagg-headers-handler by @noalimoy in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/905\r\n* deps(actions): bump crate-ci/typos from 1.45.2 to 1.46.0 in the github-actions group by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/985\r\n* refactor(test): drop igw/ prefix on integration test paths by @hexfusion in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/984\r\n* tokenizer: consolidate to a single PrepareData plugin by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/863\r\n* ci: implement Phase 1 security and reliability hardening by @Jwrede in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/960\r\n* [migrate:] phase 1 of e2e tests migration from sigs.k8s.io/gateway-api-inference-extension @ ed1af5d8 by @roytman in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/987\r\n* Enabled more lint rules and corrected related code issues - part three by @shmuelk in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/990\r\n* Refactor label-based filters: rename, deprecate, and add runtime warn… by @szedan-rh in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/842\r\n* [flake] Testing: wait for manager shutdown before releasing test cleanup by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/992\r\n* migration: Change the Group Name in the configuration pseudo CRD to llm-d.ai by @shmuelk in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/972\r\n* Updated the main readme file by @ahg-g in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/978\r\n* ci(deps): allow only patch bumps by dependabot by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/991\r\n* refactor(datalayer): simplify Collector lifecycle, add error metrics by @hexfusion in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/899\r\n* deps(go): bump the go-dependencies group across 1 directory with 3 updates by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/993\r\n* switch to `on: pull_request_target` as GH unconditionally restricts GITHUB_TOKEN to read-only for pull_request by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/994\r\n* refactor(tokenizer): return errors from PrepareRequestData instead of swallowing them by @acardace in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/998\r\n* Add PR size labeler workflow by @yehuditkerido in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/997\r\n* [sidecar] strip disaggregation headers before forwarding to model server by @andreyod in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/999\r\n* fix(e2e/epp): treat non-HTTP-200 as failure in generateTraffic by @revit13 in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1013\r\n* Added a terminology section to clarify how everything fits under llm-d Router by @ahg-g in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1016\r\n* refactor: align all import aliases by @evacchi in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/921\r\n* Re-enable two more lint rules that were disabled for the IGW migration by @shmuelk in     "body": "Here is a summary of the release notes for **v0.9.0-rc.2**:\r\n\r\n## Highlights\r\n\r\n*   **Performance Wins:** Optimizations were made to the sidecar proxy hot path for high-concurrency P/D routing ([#746](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/746)) and allocations in `Scheduler.Schedule` were reduced by ~90% on large fleets ([#1171](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1171)). OpenAI parser response usage parsing allocations were also minimized ([#1583](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1583)).\r\n*   **Key Features:** Added support for multi-modal image content in approximate prefix cache match, and encoder cache affinity scorer, Added support for the Anthropic `v1/messages` API ([#1088](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1088)), Mooncake as a new KV-connector in the routing sidecar ([#1193](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1193)), and a chunked decode feature to the sidecar ([#822](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/822)).\r\n*   **New Metrics:** Introduced inter-token latency (ITL) ([#1591](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1591)), stream-aware TPOT, and core TTFT metrics ([#1499](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1499)).\r\n*   **Major Bug Fixes:** Resolved a panic in the SGLang proxy handling concurrent requests ([#632](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/632)), fixed dropped multimodal content in prefix cache scoring/tokenizer rendering ([#781](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/781)), and patched a ZMQ port range validation issue to prevent multi-rank pod failures ([#1242](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1242)).\r\n\r\n---\r\n\r\n## Upgrade Steps & Deprecations\r\n\r\n*   **Repository & Binary Renaming:** The project has officially transitioned from `llm-d-inference-scheduler` to **`llm-d-router`**. Reflecting this change, Docker images have been renamed to `router-endpoint-picker` and `router-disagg-sidecar` ([#1098](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1098)), and configuration pseudo CRD groups are migrating to `llm-d.ai` ([#972](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/972)).\r\n*   **Removed Sidecar Flags:** The deprecated `--inference-pool-name` and `--inference-pool-namespace` flags have been permanently removed from the sidecar ([#1416](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1416)).\r\n*   **Metric Changes:** Legacy metrics feature gates and their associated CLI flags have been removed from the EPP template ([#1418](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1418), [#1466](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1466)). The metrics subsystem has also been unified and renamed to `llm_d_epp` ([#1661](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1661)).\r\n*   **Component Deprecations:** \r\n    *   The `disagg-headers-handler` has been deprecated in favor of `PreRequest` inside the `disagg-profile-handler` ([#905](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/905)).\r\n    *   The UDS-backend in `token-producer` is deprecated; configuring the vLLM render endpoint instead is highly suggested ([#1079](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1079)).\r\n*   **Istio & Gateway API:** Istio has been upgraded to **1.29.2** ([#1052](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/1052)) and the Gateway API has been updated to **v1.5.1** ([#780](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/780)). The previous workaround for vLLM Data Parallel on Istio 1.28 is now deprecated ([#727](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/727)).\r\n## What's Changed\r\n* Fix for flaky sites during lychee md link checker by @pierDipi in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/485\r\n* deps(actions): bump actions/checkout from 5 to 6 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/487\r\n* deps(go): bump google.golang.org/grpc from 1.76.0 to 1.77.0 in the go-dependencies group by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/486\r\n* Use kv-cache-manager based on Go mod version instead of hardcoded by @pierDipi in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/484\r\n* update llm-d-kv-cache version to v0.4.0 by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/492\r\n* fix: github action missing Trivy scan on sidecar image by @zdtsw in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/481\r\n* [Fix] Enhance macOS Makefile to Support Non-Homebrew Python Installations by @hyeongyun0916 in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/489\r\n* feat(allowlist): support both v1 and v1alpha2 InferencePool APIs with flag by @googs1025 in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/474\r\n* fix: make 'install-dependencies' and 'build' target by @zdtsw in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/493\r\n* skip lint and test when only docs change by @setsunakute in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/494\r\n* fix: Fixes for Data Parallel support when also running with Prefix Disaggregation by @shmuelk in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-router/pull/498\r\n* sync gie to v1.2.0 by @nirrozenbaum in     "body": "## What's Changed\r\n* [bug] fix multimodal content dropped in prefix cache scoring and tokenizer chat rendering by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/781\r\n* [Docs] Restructure development.md by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/769\r\n* deps(actions): bump actions/cache from 4 to 5 in the github-actions group by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/788\r\n* Print Pod statuses and logs if e2e tests fail by @roytman in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/783\r\n* Propagates relevant environment variables into test container by @roytman in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/785\r\n* bump kvc import to v0.7.0 by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/789\r\n* import kvc-v0.7.1 by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/796\r\n* Add tests for disaggregated Encoder. by @revit13 in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/777\r\n* (1/2) import igw v1.5.0-rc1 by @zetxqx in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/806\r\n* deps(go): bump go.opentelemetry.io/contrib/instrumentation/net/http/otelhttp from 0.67.0 to 0.68.0 in the go-dependencies group across 1 directory by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/809\r\n* deps(actions): bump crate-ci/typos from 1.44.0 to 1.45.0 in the github-actions group across 1 directory by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/801\r\n* perf: optimize sidecar proxy hot path for high-concurrency P/D routing by @tlrmchlsmth in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/746\r\n* [feat] [cicd] Use distroless/static as default runtime base image by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/795\r\n* Support image build with different base via Maefile and document runtime debugging by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/821\r\n* [cicd] Compare coverage against `main` and latest `release-*` by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/757\r\n* deps(actions): bump the github-actions group across 1 directory with 2 updates by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/824\r\n* Script to automate migration for GAIE code into llm-d by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/804\r\n* feat: Add support for Responses API by @asharkhan3101 in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/599\r\n* update image-build-builder rule by @roytman in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/830\r\n* [cicd] Add release notes action by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/791\r\n* switch active-request scorer's logging from debug to trace by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/825\r\n* Add ahg-g as an owner by @ahg-g in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/835\r\n* Refactoring plugins according to GAIE structure by @roytman in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/827\r\n* Add lint exclusions from GAIE by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/834\r\n* fix: broken link after refactoring by @zdtsw in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/839\r\n* deps(go): bump github.com/moby/spdystream from 0.5.0 to 0.5.1 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/838\r\n* migration scripts: improve tooling robustness by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/843\r\n* Update to GAIE v1.5 by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/844\r\n* fix: CVE-2025-61729 - pin toolchain to go1.25.8 by @andresllh https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/861  \r\n* use endpoints extractor for kvevents subscription mgmt in precise-prefix-cache-scorer by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/862\r\n* fix image build with Podman by @roytman in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/869\r\n\r\n## New Contributors\r\n* @revit13 made their first contribution in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/777\r\n* @zetxqx made their first contribution in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/806\r\n* @tlrmchlsmth made their first contribution in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/746\r\n* @asharkhan3101 made their first contribution in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/599\r\n\r\n**Full Changelog**: https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/compare/v0.7.1...v0.8.0",

    "body": "Inference Scheduler release v0.7.1\r\n\r\nThis patch release updates KV-Cache to v0.7.1.\r\nSee [v0.7.0 release notes](https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/releases/tag/v0.7.0).\r\n\r\nDocker images are available:\r\n```console\r\n# EPP\r\ndocker pull ghcr.io/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler:v0.7.1\r\n# sidecar\r\ndocker pull ghcr.io/llm-d/llm-d-routing-sidecar:v0.7.1\r\n```"

    "node_id": "RE_kwDOOkxMpc4SKjAh",

    "name": "v0.7.0",

    "prerelease": false,
    "created_at": "2026-04-02T16:12:13Z",
    "updated_at": "2026-04-02T16:27:40Z",

    "assets": [

    ],
    "tarball_url": "https://api.github.com/repos/llm-d/llm-d-router/tarball/v0.7.0",
    "zipball_url": "https://api.github.com/repos/llm-d/llm-d-router/zipball/v0.7.0",
    "body": "llm-d-inference-scheduler v0.7.0 Release\r\n\r\nDocker images are available:\r\n```console\r\n# EPP\r\ndocker pull ghcr.io/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler:v0.7.0\r\n# sidecar\r\ndocker pull ghcr.io/llm-d/llm-d-routing-sidecar:v0.7.0\r\n```\r\n\r\n## Highlights\r\n- Gateway API Inference Extension v1.4 (and Gateway API v1.5.1): standalone EPP, InferencePool / Helm / gRPC-related improvements, significant work in flow control and datalayer internals.\r\n- Initial EPD support (disaggregation profile, KV cache enablement).\r\n- Sidecar command line option handling has been cleaned up and simplified.\r\n- Numerous testing and developer experience improvements (e.g., new Makefile targets. elimination of CGO requirements, containerized builds, improved Podman support, automatic dev image built on every PR to main, etc.)\r\n\r\n> [!NOTE]\r\n> Trivy reports a [CVE-2026-4046 on ubi9-micro](https://access.redhat.com/security/cve/cve-2026-4046). As both inference-scheduler and sidecar executables are built with `CGO_ENABLED=0`, the binaries are fully statically linked and do not link against glibc at all. This is listed for awareness.\r\n\r\n## What's Changed\r\n* fix(ci): update Trivy to v0.69.2 by @pierDipi in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/675\r\n* Allow sidecar server to reload TLS certificates by @pierDipi in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/607\r\n* Use trivy action for image scanning by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/688\r\n* deps(go): bump the go-dependencies group with 7 updates by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/692\r\n* feat(sidecar): simplify TLS command line options with StringSlice flags by @gyliu513 in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/682\r\n* Fix terminolgy and add links to docs/archiecture.md by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/695\r\n* replace map[string]bool with map[string]struct{} by @roytman in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/696\r\n* Add presubmit makefile target for local verification before pushing a PR by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/687\r\n* Update Trivy scan to run newer version with explicit auth tokens by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/698\r\n* Remove extra trivy params by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/702\r\n* fix: simplify InferencePool flag to namespace/name format by @gyliu513 in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/685\r\n* Trivy complains of user-password mismatch by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/704\r\n* fix(test): Add unit test for pd_prerequest.go by @gyliu513 in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/706\r\n* Remove trivy cache and enable manual workflow dispatch by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/713\r\n* initial E/PD extension of the sidecar by @roytman in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/643\r\n* Check for uniqueness of media URLs by @roytman in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/717\r\n* Move typo checking from tools makefile to main, under lint by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/719\r\n* Rename EncoderPodsHeader according to other constants by @roytman in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/721\r\n* Implement Options pattern for sidecar proxy by @Mohamedma96 in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/697\r\n* Rename common constants by @roytman in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/722\r\n* deps(actions): bump dorny/paths-filter from 3 to 4 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/723\r\n* Enable major version updates to gh actions by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/714\r\n* NonCachedTokens defines the minimum number of non-cached tokens required by @modassarrana89-new in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/691\r\n* Add external tokenizer PrepareData plugin and TokenizedPrompt scorer support by @acardace in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/694\r\n* Deprecate the workaround used to support vLLM Data Parallel on Istio 1.28 by @shmuelk in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/727\r\n* build: remove CGO dependency by migrating to pure-Go ZMQ by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/728\r\n* deps(go): bump google.golang.org/grpc from 1.79.2 to 1.79.3 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/737\r\n* Use kubectl kustomize instead of standalone kustomize by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/741\r\n* Add idle pod config to active-request-scorer by @dagrayvid in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/669\r\n* [build] Optimize docker build (local and CICD) by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/740\r\n* feat: speculative indexing for PrecisePrefixCacheScorer by @bongwoobak in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/659\r\n* Remove obsolete hashBlockSize by @roytman in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/748\r\n* feat: add optional Prometheus monitoring to Kind dev environment by @hexfusion in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/742\r\n* fix: support podman-docker in e2e tests and Makefile by @hexfusion in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/730\r\n* Remove UDS tokenizer image build from inference scheduler repo by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/739\r\n* deps(go): bump the kubernetes group with 5 updates by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/754\r\n* test: add disruption e2e tests for scheduler failure scenarios by @hexfusion in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/735\r\n* Unified Disaggregate Handler by @roytman in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/732\r\n* [build] Add test coverage reporting by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/749\r\n* [refactor] Unify sidecar `Config` and `Options` by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/751\r\n* [cicd[ Make coverage comparison optional by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/762\r\n* A temporary fix to use the previous simulator image by @roytman in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/766\r\n* Combine EncodeHeaderHandler and PrefillHeaderHandler into a single DisaggHeadersHandler by @roytman in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/758\r\n* Prevent mismatch between new and deprecated APIs by @roytman in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/756\r\n* Revert IGW import to 1.4.0 and update tokenizer plugin accordingly by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/770\r\n* fix(test): Increase test coverage for prefix_based_pd_decider.go by @gyliu513 in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/715\r\n* Run unit, integration tests and builds in a container by @acardace in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/521\r\n* update Makefile to run e2e tests locally with Podman by @roytman in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/775\r\n* implement context-length-aware plugin (scorer/filter) by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/550\r\n* Build images use Go builtin cross compilation by @shmuelk in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/776\r\n* feat: bump llm-d-kv-cache for MM-aware prefix-cache routing by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/772\r\n* change simulator version to v0.8.1 by @mayabar in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/773\r\n* [fix] [cicd] Add missing newline at end of OWNERS, make script more robust for missing newlines by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/779\r\n* [fix] Added custom LevelEncoder for sidecar by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/778\r\n* Update GW API to v1.5.1 by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/780\r\n* Add E2E_KEEP_CLUSTER_ON_FAILURE env variable by @roytman in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/782\r\n* bump kvc import to v0.7.0 by @vMaroon https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/789\r\n\r\n## New Contributors\r\n* @Mohamedma96 made their first contribution in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/697\r\n* @modassarrana89-new made their first contribution in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/691\r\n* @dagrayvid made their first contribution in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/669\r\n* @hexfusion made their first contribution in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/742\r\n\r\n**Full Changelog**: https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/compare/v0.6.0...v0.7.0",
    "mentions_count": 14
  },
