@shmuelk in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/727\r\n* build: remove CGO dependency by migrating to pure-Go ZMQ by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/728\r\n* deps(go): bump google.golang.org/grpc from 1.79.2 to 1.79.3 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/737\r\n* Use kubectl kustomize instead of standalone kustomize by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/741\r\n* Add idle pod config to active-request-scorer by @dagrayvid in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/669\r\n* [build] Optimize docker build (local and CICD) by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/740\r\n* feat: speculative indexing for PrecisePrefixCacheScorer by @bongwoobak in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/659\r\n* Remove obsolete hashBlockSize by @roytman in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/748\r\n* feat: add optional Prometheus monitoring to Kind dev environment by @hexfusion in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/742\r\n* fix: support podman-docker in e2e tests and Makefile by @hexfusion in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/730\r\n* Remove UDS tokenizer image build from inference scheduler repo by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/739\r\n* deps(go): bump the kubernetes group with 5 updates by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/754\r\n* test: add disruption e2e tests for scheduler failure scenarios by @hexfusion in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/735\r\n* Unified Disaggregate Handler by @roytman in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/732\r\n* [build] Add test coverage reporting by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/749\r\n* [refactor] Unify sidecar `Config` and `Options` by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/751\r\n* [cicd[ Make coverage comparison optional by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/762\r\n* A temporary fix to use the previous simulator image by @roytman in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/766\r\n* Combine EncodeHeaderHandler and PrefillHeaderHandler into a single DisaggHeadersHandler by @roytman in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/758\r\n* Prevent mismatch between new and deprecated APIs by @roytman in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/756\r\n* Revert IGW import to 1.4.0 and update tokenizer plugin accordingly by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/770\r\n* fix(test): Increase test coverage for prefix_based_pd_decider.go by @gyliu513 in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/715\r\n* Run unit, integration tests and builds in a container by @acardace in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/521\r\n* update Makefile to run e2e tests locally with Podman by @roytman in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/775\r\n* implement context-length-aware plugin (scorer/filter) by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/550\r\n* Build images use Go builtin cross compilation by @shmuelk in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/776\r\n* feat: bump llm-d-kv-cache for MM-aware prefix-cache routing by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/772\r\n* change simulator version to v0.8.1 by @mayabar in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/773\r\n* [fix] [cicd] Add missing newline at end of OWNERS, make script more robust for missing newlines by @elevran in API to v1.5.1 by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/780\r\n* [bug] fix multimodal content dropped in prefix cache scoring and tokenizer chat rendering by @vMaroon https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/781\r\n* Add E2E_KEEP_CLUSTER_ON_FAILURE env variable by @roytman in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/782\r\n\r\n## New     "body": "Docker images are available: \r\n```bash\r\n# EPP\r\ndocker pull ghcr.io/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler:v0.6.0\r\n# sidecar\r\ndocker pull ghcr.io/llm-d/llm-d-routing-sidecar:v0.6.0\r\n```\r\n\r\n## Notable\r\n* Use Go 1.25.x\r\n* Updated dependencies (IGW v1.3.1, KV-cache v0.6.0)\r\n* OpenTelemetry instrumentation in scheduler and sidecar\r\n* Support for `/v1/models` attributes on endpoints\r\n* [bug] SGLang proxy handling of concurrent requests\r\n* \"Decode first\" routing sidecar flow\r\n* Flexible P/D decisions\r\n* Support (optional) use of tokenization sidecar\r\n\r\n## What's Changed\r\n* free disk space on ci-release by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/587\r\n* feat: use Tinyllama as the \"model\" for kind test and switch to use precise-prefix-cache-score in config by @zdtsw in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/581\r\n* Update linter configuration by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/588\r\n* fix: config should use new precise-prefix-cache-scorer by @zdtsw in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/576\r\n* deps(actions): bump crate-ci/typos from 1.42.1 to 1.42.2 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/589\r\n* Updated to more recent GIE by @shmuelk in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/592\r\n* pull kvc v0.5.0 libs by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/595\r\n* deps(actions): bump crate-ci/typos from 1.42.2 to 1.43.0 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/596\r\n* Address return nil,nil linter error in test mock by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/598\r\n* deps(go): bump the go-dependencies group with 2 updates by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/597\r\n* Models extractor by @irar2 in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/553\r\n* feat(lmcache): implement decode first flow on lmcache connector when cache_hit_threshold field is present by @kyanokashi in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/509\r\n* Extend support for different ways to decide if disaggregated PD is required by @mayabar in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/531\r\n* chore: fix wrong port for NIXL by @zdtsw in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/593\r\n* fix: resolve JSON serialization error in active-request-scorer debug logs by @albertoperdomo2 in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/602\r\n* Match documentation with default model in scripts by @shmuelk in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/615\r\n* Cleanup End to End test framework by @shmuelk in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/625\r\n* deps(go): bump the kubernetes group with 5 updates by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/629\r\n* deps(go): bump google.golang.org/grpc from 1.78.0 to 1.79.1 in the go-dependencies group by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/628\r\n* deps(actions): bump crate-ci/typos from 1.43.0 to 1.43.5 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/627\r\n* Update Go to Go 1 25 by @shmuelk in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/624\r\n* feat: update to use new kv-cache UDS tokenizer by @zdtsw in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/609\r\n* fix: Add kustomization file for rbac by @albertoperdomo2 in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/601\r\n* Fix panic in SGLang proxy handling of concurrent requests by @yangligt2 in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/632\r\n* Add otel tracing instrumentation by @sallyom in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/506\r\n* bump kvc import to v0.5.1-rc2 by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/657\r\n* pull in v0.6.0 of kvcache by @Gregory-Pereira in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/660\r\n* deps(go): bump go.opentelemetry.io/otel/sdk from 1.39.0 to 1.40.0 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/670\r\n\r\n## New Contributors\r\n* @albertoperdomo2 made their first contribution in     "body": "Docker image is available at: \r\n```bash\r\ndocker pull ghcr.io/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler:v0.5.0\r\n```\r\n\r\n## Notable\r\n* Prefill/Decode disaggregation awareness in filters and scorers\r\n* Support for data parallel serving validated with vLLM and inference-sim\r\n* Various CICD enhancements\r\n* IGW: Flow control scale from/to zero support\r\n* IGW: Standalone EPP\r\n\r\n## What's Changed\r\n* Fix for flaky sites during lychee md link checker by @pierDipi in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/485\r\n* deps(actions): bump actions/checkout from 5 to 6 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/487\r\n* deps(go): bump google.golang.org/grpc from 1.76.0 to 1.77.0 in the go-dependencies group by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/486\r\n* Use kv-cache-manager based on Go mod version instead of hardcoded by @pierDipi in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/484\r\n* update llm-d-kv-cache version to v0.4.0 by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/492\r\n* fix: github action missing Trivy scan on sidecar image by @zdtsw in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/481\r\n* [Fix] Enhance macOS Makefile to Support Non-Homebrew Python Installations by @hyeongyun0916 in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/489\r\n* feat(allowlist): support both v1 and v1alpha2 InferencePool APIs with flag by @googs1025 in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/474\r\n* fix: make 'install-dependencies' and 'build' target by @zdtsw in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/493\r\n* skip lint and test when only docs change by @setsunakute in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/494\r\n* fix: Fixes for Data Parallel support when also running with Prefix Disaggregation by @shmuelk in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/498\r\n* sync gie to v1.2.0 by @nirrozenbaum in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/499\r\n* Error if PYTHON_CONFIG is empty by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/497\r\n* Add GH action to check for signed and verified commits in PR by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/500\r\n* deps(actions): bump crate-ci/typos from 1.39.2 to 1.40.0 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/501\r\n* build: make build should use CGO_CFLAGS, CGO_LDFLAGS by @evacchi in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/503\r\n* chore: bump gie to v1.2.1 by @nirrozenbaum in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/504\r\n* deps(go): bump sigs.k8s.io/gateway-api from 1.4.0 to 1.4.1 in the kubernetes group by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/508\r\n* deps(go): bump the go-dependencies group with 3 updates by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/507\r\n* Miscellaneous dependency updates by @shmuelk in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/510\r\n* deps(go): bump the kubernetes group with 5 updates by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/513\r\n* Fix running `make env-dev-kind` by @acardace in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/512\r\n* test: add precise_prefix_cache_test by @evacchi in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/505\r\n* test: reuse upstream data store and enable logr in unit tests by @MregXN in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/518\r\n* feat: allow pd_profile_handler to handle diverse plugin types by @hyeongyun0916 in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/516\r\n* deps(actions): bump crate-ci/typos from 1.40.0 to 1.40.1 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/526\r\n* deps(go): bump google.golang.org/grpc from 1.77.0 to 1.78.0 in the go-dependencies group by @dependabot[bot] in set python version and pin docker image with tag by @zdtsw in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/543\r\n* chore(test): update API version for nixl test by @zdtsw in make no-hit-lru P/D-aware by @evacchi in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/522\r\n* Update disaggregated Prefill/Decode inference serving documentation by @mayabar in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/571\r\n* deps(actions): bump crate-ci/typos from 1.42.0 to 1.42.1 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/572\r\n* deps(go): bump github.com/onsi/ginkgo/v2 from 2.27.4 to 2.27.5 in the go-dependencies group by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/573\r\n* fix reviewers auto assign minor bug by @nirrozenbaum in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/575\r\n* fix(scorer): make active request pd aware by @kyanokashi in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/569\r\n* test(e2e): cleanup kind cluster by @zdtsw in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/563\r\n* refactor: add early validation in DP profile handler by @zdtsw in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/554\r\n* deps(go): bump the kubernetes group with 2 updates by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/574\r\n* refactor: kv cache manager repo by @sagearc in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/570\r\n* bumping IGW version to the full released version by @kfswain in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/583\r\n* Enable prefix-cache awareness in active-active multi-replica scheduler deployments by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/578\r\n* Switch to pre-built vLLM wheels for CPU builds by @sagearc in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/582\r\n* update llm-d-kv-cache import to v0.5.0-RC1 by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/584\r\n* Use 1.3.0 CRDs by @shmuelk in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/586\r\n\r\n### Updates in Inference Gateway Extension v1.3.0\r\n\r\nllm-d-inference-scheduler v0.5.0 has been updated to use the latest version of the Inference Gateway Extension which is 1.3.0.\r\nYou can see those changes [here](https://github.com/kubernetes-sigs/gateway-api-inference-extension/releases/tag/v1.3.0)\r\n\r\n## New Contributors\r\n* @setsunakute made their first contribution in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/494\r\n* @evacchi made their first contribution in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/503\r\n* @acardace made their first contribution in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/512\r\n* @MregXN made their first contribution in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/518\r\n* @davidbreitgand made their first contribution in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/525\r\n* @kyanokashi made their first contribution in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/569\r\n* @sagearc made their first contribution in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/570\r\n\r\n**Full Changelog**: https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/compare/v0.4.0...v0.5.0",

    "body": "## What's Changed\r\n* Fix for flaky sites during lychee md link checker by @pierDipi in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/485\r\n* deps(actions): bump actions/checkout from 5 to 6 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/487\r\n* deps(go): bump google.golang.org/grpc from 1.76.0 to 1.77.0 in the go-dependencies group by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/486\r\n* Use kv-cache-manager based on Go mod version instead of hardcoded by @pierDipi in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/484\r\n* update llm-d-kv-cache version to v0.4.0 by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/492\r\n* fix: github action missing Trivy scan on sidecar image by @zdtsw in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/481\r\n* [Fix] Enhance macOS Makefile to Support Non-Homebrew Python Installations by @hyeongyun0916 in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/489\r\n* feat(allowlist): support both v1 and v1alpha2 InferencePool APIs with flag by @googs1025 in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/474\r\n* fix: make 'install-dependencies' and 'build' target by @zdtsw in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/493\r\n* skip lint and test when only docs change by @setsunakute in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/494\r\n* fix: Fixes for Data Parallel support when also running with Prefix Disaggregation by @shmuelk in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/498\r\n* sync gie to v1.2.0 by @nirrozenbaum in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/499\r\n* Error if PYTHON_CONFIG is empty by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/497\r\n* Add GH action to check for signed and verified commits in PR by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/500\r\n* deps(actions): bump crate-ci/typos from 1.39.2 to 1.40.0 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/501\r\n* build: make build should use CGO_CFLAGS, CGO_LDFLAGS by @evacchi in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/503\r\n* chore: bump gie to v1.2.1 by @nirrozenbaum in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/504\r\n* deps(go): bump sigs.k8s.io/gateway-api from 1.4.0 to 1.4.1 in the kubernetes group by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/508\r\n* deps(go): bump the go-dependencies group with 3 updates by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/507\r\n* Miscellaneous dependency updates by @shmuelk in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/510\r\n* deps(go): bump the kubernetes group with 5 updates by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/513\r\n* Fix running `make env-dev-kind` by @acardace in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/512\r\n* test: add precise_prefix_cache_test by @evacchi in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/505\r\n* test: reuse upstream data store and enable logr in unit tests by @MregXN in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/518\r\n* feat: allow pd_profile_handler to handle diverse plugin types by @hyeongyun0916 in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/516\r\n* deps(actions): bump crate-ci/typos from 1.40.0 to 1.40.1 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/526\r\n* deps(go): bump google.golang.org/grpc from 1.77.0 to 1.78.0 in the go-dependencies group by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/527\r\n* feat(metrics): add model_name label to PD decision metric by @googs1025 in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/528\r\n* deps(actions): bump crate-ci/typos from 1.40.1 to 1.41.0 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/532\r\n* Configure dependabot ignores Go version updates by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/533\r\n* Updates the architecture description  by @davidbreitgand in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/525\r\n* Dependabot: exert finer control over package updates by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/542\r\n* port auto-assign action from llm-d-kv-cache by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/551\r\n* refactor: set python version and pin docker image with tag by @zdtsw in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/543\r\n* chore(test): update API version for nixl test by @zdtsw in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/555\r\n* deps(go): bump the go-dependencies group with 2 updates by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/558\r\n* deps(actions): bump crate-ci/typos from 1.41.0 to 1.42.0 by @dependabot[bot] in commit message by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/561\r\n* bump gie to v1.3.0 rc2 by @nirrozenbaum in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/562\r\n* Update OWNERS by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/559\r\n* refactor: Makefile, update docs by @zdtsw in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/463\r\n* feat: add metrics validation in e2e test by @googs1025 in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/529\r\n* feat: make no-hit-lru P/D-aware by @evacchi in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/522\r\n* Update disaggregated Prefill/Decode inference serving documentation by @mayabar in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/571\r\n* deps(actions): bump crate-ci/typos from 1.42.0 to 1.42.1 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/572\r\n* deps(go): bump github.com/onsi/ginkgo/v2 from 2.27.4 to 2.27.5 in the go-dependencies group by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/573\r\n* fix reviewers auto assign minor bug by @nirrozenbaum in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/575\r\n* fix(scorer): make active request pd aware by @kyanokashi in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/569\r\n* test(e2e): cleanup kind cluster by @zdtsw in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/563\r\n* refactor: add early validation in DP profile handler by @zdtsw in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/554\r\n* deps(go): bump the kubernetes group with 2 updates by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/574\r\n* refactor: kv cache manager repo by @sagearc in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/570\r\n* bumping IGW version to the full released version by @kfswain in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/583\r\n* Enable prefix-cache awareness in active-active multi-replica scheduler deployments by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/578\r\n* Switch to pre-built vLLM wheels for CPU builds by @sagearc in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/582\r\n* update llm-d-kv-cache import to v0.5.0-RC1 by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/584\r\n* Use 1.3.0 CRDs by @shmuelk in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/586\r\n\r\n### Updates in Inference Gateway Extension v1.3.0\r\n\r\nllm-d-inference-scheduler v0.5.0 has been updated to use the latest version of the Inference Gateway Extension which is 1.3.0.\r\nYou can see those changes     "body": "Docker image is available at: \r\n```bash\r\ndocker pull ghcr.io/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler:v0.4.0\r\n```\r\n\r\n## What's Changed\r\n* Use a production version of Istio by @shmuelk in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/334\r\n* add vMaroon as code owner by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/342\r\n* Upgrade `github.com/llm-d/llm-d-kv-cache-manager` import to `v0.3.0` by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/344\r\n* add a hold label when PRs are pushed to branch other than main by @nirrozenbaum in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/345\r\n* sync gic to latest v1.0.0 release by @nirrozenbaum in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/353\r\n* deps(actions): bump actions/stale from 9 to 10 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/350\r\n* deps(actions): bump actions/setup-go from 5 to 6 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/351\r\n* deps(actions): bump crate-ci/typos from 1.35.7 to 1.36.2 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/348\r\n* deps(go): bump the go-dependencies group with 7 updates by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/349\r\n* bump llm-d-kv-cache-manager version by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/359\r\n* fix: Rename config to `kv-cache-utilization-scorer` from `kv-cache-scorer` by @yankay in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/358\r\n* updating release issue-template by @kfswain in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/361\r\n* bump llm-d-kv-cache-manager version (v0.3.2) by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/365\r\n* deps(go): bump github.com/onsi/ginkgo/v2 from 2.25.3 to 2.26.0 in the go-dependencies group by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/368\r\n* feat: Add a scoring plugin to distribute new groups evenly by @usize in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/357\r\n* implement PreRequest and PostResponse interface checks by @learner0810 in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/372\r\n* deps(go): bump the kubernetes group with 2 updates by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/369\r\n* deps(go): bump google.golang.org/grpc from 1.75.1 to 1.76.0 in the go-dependencies group by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/374\r\n* Supports the ResponseComplete plugin by @learner0810 in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/378\r\n* deps(actions): bump crate-ci/typos from 1.36.2 to 1.38.1 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/373\r\n* Fix multi-architecture image issues with Kind by @shmuelk in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/362\r\n* feat: Moved the Routing Sidecar from its own repo to the inference-scheduler repo by @shmuelk in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/379\r\n* Upgrade to use Gateway Inference Extension 1.1.0 rc.1 by @shmuelk in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/384\r\n* deps(go): bump github.com/onsi/ginkgo/v2 from 2.26.0 to 2.27.1 in the go-dependencies group by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/389\r\n* Ensure that max_completion_tokens=1 in Prefill by @shmuelk in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/403\r\n* Add explanation of inference-scheduler relation to IGW/GIE by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/393\r\n* Add test coverage to test-unit Makefile target by @carlory in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/391\r\n* Add regression tests for max_completion_tokens by @pierDipi in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/411\r\n* Makefile refactoring to minimize the number of targets by @shmuelk in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/397\r\n* feat: Add vLLM Data Parallel support to llm-d-inference-scheduler by @shmuelk in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/392\r\n* fix(scorer): prevent potential division by zero in ActiveRequest.Score by @googs1025 in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/413\r\n* Fixed wildcard targets by @shmuelk in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/416\r\n* deps(actions): bump     "body": "In addition to the below changes these patches include fixes to the kv-cache-manager dependency\r\n\r\n## What's Changed\r\n* bump gie version to v0.5.0 by @nirrozenbaum in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/256\r\n* Fixes for Steps to Build a Kubernetes Development Environment by @dumb0002 in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/259\r\n* bump gie version to v0.5.1 rc1 (patch release) by @nirrozenbaum in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/262\r\n* added badges to readme by @nirrozenbaum in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/261\r\n* chore: bump gie version by @nirrozenbaum in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/263\r\n* Update `prefix-cache-scorer` Configuration Doc Entry by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/264\r\n* Update Tokenizer Release Version by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/265\r\n* #245 Followup - Makefile Installs `libzmq` Dependency by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/266\r\n* deps(go): bump the go-dependencies group with 3 updates by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/257\r\n* Add codespell integration for spell checking by @Jooho in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/221\r\n* Initial CODEOWNERS file by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/267\r\n* added issues templates by @nirrozenbaum in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/272\r\n* deps(actions): bump crate-ci/typos from 1.34.0 to 1.35.1 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/275\r\n* small updates to documentation by @kfswain in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/277\r\n* Change CI to only create a latest tagged image on releases by @shmuelk in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/278\r\n* fix: correct shell command substitution syntax in Makefile by @yankay in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/276\r\n* add reference to writing a new plugin by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/280\r\n* deps(actions): bump crate-ci/typos from 1.35.1 to 1.35.3 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/282\r\n* deps(actions): bump actions/checkout from 4 to 5 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/283\r\n* Remove non-existent labels from dependabot configuration by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/285\r\n* chore: ⬆️ bump components gie to v0.5.1. by @yafengio in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/288\r\n* Add workflows to automate aged issues management by @anoruxylene in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/289\r\n* update load aware scorer unit tests by @jairuigou in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/291\r\n* Add build metadata to Docker image by @carlory in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/292\r\n* Add Prow GitHub Actions by @Jooho in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/290\r\n* remove setup log by @carlory in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/294\r\n* OWNERS_ALIASES is not working with current automation by @nirrozenbaum in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/296\r\n* deps(actions): bump crate-ci/typos from 1.35.3 to 1.35.4 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/299\r\n* deps(actions): bump actions/checkout from 4 to 5 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/300\r\n* deps(go): bump the go-dependencies group with 2 updates by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/301\r\n* fix: Makefile fixes for MacOS by @shmuelk in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/304\r\n* Update comment in prow-pr-automerge.yml by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/303\r\n* Remove github CODEOWNERS by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/306\r\n* deps(docker): bump projectquay/golang from 1.24 to 1.25 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/302\r\n* Support for installation of inference-sim with kv-cache enabled by @mayabar in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/305\r\n* add tokenizer directory to simulator deployment yaml by @mayabar in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/307\r\n* [feature] Added Active-Request-Scorer by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/297\r\n* deps(actions): bump crate-ci/typos from 1.35.4 to 1.35.5 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/316\r\n* deps(go): bump github.com/stretchr/testify from 1.10.0 to 1.11.0 in the go-dependencies group by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/314\r\n* chore: Added end to end tests by @shmuelk in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/310\r\n* ByLabelSelector filter tests by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/315\r\n* chore: drop plugin type from types and file by @yyzxw in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/308\r\n* fixed a bug where typos check is checking go.mod and go.sum by @nirrozenbaum in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/321\r\n* minor tweaks to Makefile by @nirrozenbaum in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/318\r\n* Split the `prefix-cache-scorer` plugins by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/323\r\n* Makefile fixes by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/322\r\n* deps(actions): bump crate-ci/typos from 1.35.5 to 1.35.7 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/325\r\n* deps(go): bump the go-dependencies group with 9 updates by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/326\r\n* Reduce img size by cleaning dnf cache by @rawagner in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/328\r\n* added comment to stale issues by @nirrozenbaum in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/327\r\n* fixed missing dependencies in makefile by @nirrozenbaum in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/330\r\n* sync with IGW release 1.0.0-rc by @nirrozenbaum in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/320\r\n* fix: make env-dev-kind fails after sync with IGW 1.0.0-rc-3 by @shmuelk in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/331\r\n\r\n## New Contributors\r\n* @dumb0002 made their first contribution in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/259\r\n* @yafengio made their first contribution in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/288\r\n* @anoruxylene made their first contribution in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/289\r\n* @jairuigou made their first contribution in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/291\r\n* @yyzxw made their first contribution in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/308\r\n* @rawagner made their first contribution in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/328\r\n\r\n**Full Changelog**: https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/compare/v0.2.1...v0.3.2",

    "body": "Small patch updating kv cache manager dependency to include support in v0.3\r\n\r\nSee the full v0.3 changes here:\r\n\r\n## What's Changed\r\n* bump gie version to v0.5.0 by     "body": "Image pull example: `docker pull ghcr.io/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler:v0.3.0`\r\n\r\n## What's Changed\r\n* bump gie version to v0.5.0 by @nirrozenbaum in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/256\r\n* Fixes for Steps to Build a Kubernetes Development Environment by @dumb0002 in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/259\r\n* bump gie version to v0.5.1 rc1 (patch release) by @nirrozenbaum in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/262\r\n* added badges to readme by @nirrozenbaum in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/261\r\n* chore: bump gie version by @nirrozenbaum in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/263\r\n* Update `prefix-cache-scorer` Configuration Doc Entry by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/264\r\n* Update Tokenizer Release Version by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/265\r\n* #245 Followup - Makefile Installs `libzmq` Dependency by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/266\r\n* deps(go): bump the go-dependencies group with 3 updates by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/257\r\n* Add codespell integration for spell checking by @Jooho in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/221\r\n* Initial CODEOWNERS file by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/267\r\n* added issues templates by @nirrozenbaum in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/272\r\n* deps(actions): bump crate-ci/typos from 1.34.0 to 1.35.1 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/275\r\n* small updates to documentation by @kfswain in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/277\r\n* Change CI to only create a latest tagged image on releases by @shmuelk in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/278\r\n* fix: correct shell command substitution syntax in Makefile by @yankay in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/276\r\n* add reference to writing a new plugin by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/280\r\n* deps(actions): bump crate-ci/typos from 1.35.1 to 1.35.3 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/282\r\n* deps(actions): bump actions/checkout from 4 to 5 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/283\r\n* Remove non-existent labels from dependabot configuration by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/285\r\n* chore: ⬆️ bump components gie to v0.5.1. by @yafengio in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/288\r\n* Add workflows to automate aged issues management by @anoruxylene in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/289\r\n* update load aware scorer unit tests by @jairuigou in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/291\r\n* Add build metadata to Docker image by @carlory in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/292\r\n* Add Prow GitHub Actions by @Jooho in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/290\r\n* remove setup log by @carlory in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/294\r\n* OWNERS_ALIASES is not working with current automation by @nirrozenbaum in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/296\r\n* deps(actions): bump crate-ci/typos from 1.35.3 to 1.35.4 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/299\r\n* deps(actions): bump actions/checkout from 4 to 5 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/300\r\n* deps(go): bump the go-dependencies group with 2 updates by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/301\r\n* fix: Makefile fixes for MacOS by @shmuelk in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/304\r\n* Update comment in prow-pr-automerge.yml by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/303\r\n* Remove github CODEOWNERS by @elevran in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/306\r\n* deps(docker): bump projectquay/golang from 1.24 to 1.25 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/302\r\n* Support for installation of inference-sim with kv-cache enabled by @mayabar in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/305\r\n* add tokenizer directory to simulator deployment yaml by @mayabar in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/307\r\n* [feature] Added Active-Request-Scorer by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/297\r\n* deps(actions): bump crate-ci/typos from 1.35.4 to 1.35.5 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/316\r\n* deps(go): bump github.com/stretchr/testify from 1.10.0 to 1.11.0 in the go-dependencies group by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/308\r\n* fixed a bug where typos check is checking go.mod and go.sum by @nirrozenbaum in `prefix-cache-scorer` plugins by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/323\r\n* Makefile fixes by @vMaroon in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/322\r\n* deps(actions): bump crate-ci/typos from 1.35.5 to 1.35.7 by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/325\r\n* deps(go): bump the go-dependencies group with 9 updates by @dependabot[bot] in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/330\r\n* sync with IGW release 1.0.0-rc by @nirrozenbaum in https://github.com/llm-d/llm-d-inference-scheduler/pull/320\r\n* fix: make
